Line search: Objective f(a)=max b (or J=2n max b). We can perform steepest descent with normalized gradient and sufficient decrease condition for subgradient: f(a - α g) ≤ f(a) - c α ||g||^2? Better: Use FISTA on smooth LSE: f_beta(a)=LSE(beta b)/beta = max approximation. It's smooth, gradient as above with softmax weights. We can use Lipschitz estimate. However LSE is always ≥max, optimizing LSE may not reduce max directly but high beta. We can update best whenever max improves.

We can combine:
- Smooth descent with LSE beta increasing, plus step via backtracking on exact max (instead of fixed). This ensures exact max nonincreasing if step accepted on max? If we use LSE gradient, exact max not guaranteed but likely. We can perform line search on exact max with small step; accept if improves.
- Or use exact subgradient: weights w uniform over top active peaks (or weights of excess). Direction d = -P(g) where g=grad of max active. Use backtracking on exact max. This is simpler and avoids LSE overflow.

Active weights: We can select active peaks as all k with b_k ≥ θ max, where θ from 1 to 0.5. Weights maybe (b_k - threshold) positive normalized, to focus on highest. I think excess weights is good.

High-res local optimizer idea:
```
def local_search(a, time_limit, rng):
  a=normalize(a,1)
  best=maxb, besta
  beta=10
  y=a.copy(); t=1
  for it in range(max):
    b=conv(y)
    M=b.max()
    if M<best: best=M; besta=y
    # weights: top/excess
    w=make_weights(b, M, phase)
    grad=grad_from_w(y,w)
    # normalize grad to have norm maybe 1
    # projected direction: d = P(y - step*grad) - y
    # line search steps: try [0.5,0.1,0.01,0.001]* base
```
Need choose step scaling. Objective max b is O(1/n). Gradient b scale? If a sum=1, b ~1/n. grad = derivative wrt a; if weights sum 1, grad ~ 2 * average a ~2/n. Norm ~ sqrt(n)*(2/n)=2/√n. Moving a changes b by O(1/n). We need step in a of order? To change M relative 1%, maybe step~0.1? But a sum=1, a_i~1/n ~3e-5. Step norm 0.1 relative huge. Need scale.

Let's compute: a_i~1/n. To reduce b (M~1/n) by say 0.01/n (100x objective? objective J=2nM, reduction 0.02 absolute from 2 to 1.98), need adjust a_i by maybe? dq≈2g·d, g~? For active weight at peak, g_i =2a_{k-i} ~2/n. Norm of g ~ sqrt(number active)*(2/n). If active top ~n points, norm ~2. Wait if w uniform over n peaks, g_i≈2/n constant? Norm ~2/√n? Actually constant 2/n over n entries norm=2/√n ~0.0002 for n=1e5? No 2/√32768=0.011. If w only peak, g_i=2a_{lag-i}; norm 2∥a∥2 ~2√(1/n)=0.008. So gradient norm small. Direction after projection has norm similar. To change b by 1e-4/n? b~3e-5; to change objective 0.001 -> M change 0.001/(2n)=1.5e-8. Gradient * step ~ step*0.01 =1.5e-8 -> step=1e-6. Very small in l2. But a_i magnitudes 3e-5; relative 3%. Step norm relative l2? ∥a∥2~1/√n=0.0055; step=1e-6 relative 0.02%. So steps can be tiny. Need scale gradient to unit norm perhaps. If d = -P(grad)/∥Pgrad∥ * radii, objective changes by radii*∥grad∥. We can set radii adaptively.

FISTA with Lipschitz: Hessian of q? b quadratic. Spectral norm of Hessian for active weights maybe ~4? Since a sum? For LSE beta, Lipschitz ~2 beta? Let's derive. The gradient of b_k wrt a is 2 (shifted a) norm ≤2∥a∥∞? No as operator, Hessian of q_k has norm 2∥a? not. For LSE with β, gradient = Σ λ_k grad b_k. Jacobian bounded by 2β? ```
def proj_subgrad_optimize(a, max_time, accel=True, weights_mode='excess', alpha=0.01):
  a=normalize(a,1); y=a.copy(); step=1e-3
  best=inf; t=1
  for it:
    b=conv(y); M=b.max(); update
    w=weights(b,M)
    g=grad(w,y)
    normg=norm(g); if normg<1e-15 break
    d=-g/normg # unit direction? Then search radii.
    # Try radii: step_current (in unit direction), step_current/2,... maybe up to 10
    # We want x=project(y + r*d, 1)
    found=False
    for r in [step, step*0.5, ..., step*8?]:
       x=project(y + r*d)
       Mx=conv(x).max()
       if Mx < M*(1-1e-12): accept; step=r*1.2?; x_new=x; found=True; break
    if not found:
       # try pure FISTA/nudge with smaller step? or random perturbation
       x=project(y + step*0.1*d); if improves accept else step*=0.5; maybe add noise.
    if accel: t update y = x_new + ...
```

Gradient:
```
def grad_weights(a, w, fsize=None):
    # w length 2n-1 or L; pad to fsize
    if len(w) < fsize: pad
    A=rfft(a,fsize); W=rfft(w, fsize)
    g = irfft(W * np.conj(A), fsize)[:n]
    return 2.0*g
```

Exact projection implement:
```
def project_simplex(v,S):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    # Remove mean? Standard algorithm with S.
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u > (css - S)/np.arange(1,v.size+1))[0][-1]+1
    theta=(css[rho-1]-S)/rho
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
Works.

But if we fast path, ensure exact sum. If v.min()>=0, return v*(S/v.sum()). This is project only if all nonnegative; projection onto simplex with nonnegative v is just scaling to sum S. Correct. If min slightly negative and sum positive, exact sort. Good.

Weights for p objective:
```
def p_weights(b, p, M):
    if p==1: return np.ones_like(b)/len(b)
    ratio=b/M
    # for p-1 exponent, if M==0 return ones
    w = ratio ** (p-1)
    # if underflow all zeros? use top
    ws=w.sum()
    if ws<1e-300: w=np.zeros_like(b); idx=np.argmax(b); w[idx]=1
    else w/=ws
    return w
```
For p=2, ratio**1 = b/M. Good.

def optimize(a, time_limit, p_start=2, p_end=32, use_momentum=False, verbose=False):
  n=len(a); a=normalize(a,1); 
  best=M; best_a=a; 
  y=a.copy(); v=0; t=1; step=1e-3/np.sqrt(n) maybe
  it=0
  while time < deadline:
    p = schedule based on progress (p_start to p_end with exp)
    b=conv(y); M=b.max(); update
    w=p_weights(b,p,M)
    g=grad_weights(y,w)
    # remove mean
    gm = g - g.mean()
    gn=np.linalg.norm(gm)
    if gn==0: break
    d=-gm/gn
    # line search
    accepted=False
    # candidate radii generated around current step. Since d unit, step is a l2 radius.
    # start step maybe 10 ** (log10(0.1) - progress?)? We'll keep adaptive.
    for mult in [1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 2.0, 4.0?]:
        r=step*mult
        x_temp = y + r*d
        x = project_simplex_fast(x_temp,1.0)
        Mx=conv(x).max()
        if Mx < M - 1e-15*max(1,M):
           accepted=True; x_new=x; step*=1.2 maybe; break
    if not accepted:
       # try last smaller with projected maybe or noise
       r=step*0.03125
       x=project(y+r*d); Mx=conv(x).max(); if Mx<M accept else step*=0.5
       if no accept and stuck>... add noise and reset.
    if use_momentum: update t+y

Line search details robust:
```
def optimize_sequence(a_init, max_time, rng=None, p_start=2.0, p_end=64.0, use_momentum=True, max_iters=1000000, verbose=False):
    if max_time <=0: return normalize, inf
    n=len(a_init); a=normalize_sum(a_init,1.0)
    fsize=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()  # if 2*n-1? size=2n-1; next pow2 = 1 << (size-1).bit_length()
    best_M=conv(a).max(); best_a=a.copy()
    y=a.copy(); prev_x=a.copy(); t=1.0
    step=0.05/np.sqrt(n)
    last_improve_it=0; it=0; start=time.monotonic()
    eval_count=0
    while elapsed<max_time:
       it+=1
       b=conv(y); M=b.max()
       if M<best_M: best_M=M; best_a=y.copy(); last_improve=it
       if it-last_improve > 200: # stuck
           # perturb best or y with noise and reset
           noise_scale=0.02/np.sqrt(n)*(1-elapsed/max_time)
           y = project_simplex(best_a + rng.normal(0, noise_scale, n),1)
           prev_x=y.copy(); t=1; step=0.05/np.sqrt(n)
           continue
       progress=(elapsed/max_time)
       p=p_start*(p_end/p_start)**progress
       # maybe if progress small p=2; if large p=32
       w=make_weights(b,p,M)
       g=grad_from_weights(y,w,fsize)
       g = g - np.mean(g)
       gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gn < 1e-15: break
       d=-g/gn
       accepted=False; new_x=None; new_step=step
       # Try radii: current, half, quarter, eighth, maybe double if progress late?
       for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 2.0, 4.0):
           r=step*mult
           x_raw=y + r*d
           x=project_simplex_fast(x_raw,1.0)
           Mx=conv(x).max()
           if Mx < M - 1e-14:
               accepted=True; new_x=x; new_step=step*mult if mult>=1 else step*mult; # update step to accepted radius
               # If accepted with mult large, increase further
               if mult==1.0: new_step=step*1.2
               elif mult in (2.0,4.0): new_step=step*mult*1.1
               else: new_step=step*mult
               break
       if not accepted:
           step *= 0.5
           # try a very tiny projected step just to move? Or break if step too small
           if step < 1e-12/np.sqrt(n): break
           # maybe add small noise to direction
           continue
       # update with momentum
       if use_momentum:
           t_new=0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4*t*t))
           y_new = new_x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(new_x - prev_x)
           y_new = np.maximum(y_new,0)  # but sum? project?
           y = project_simplex_fast(y_new,1.0)
           # Ensure sum; no monotonic maybe; if conv(y)>best_M maybe still okay
           prev_x = new_x
           t=t_new
       else:
           y=new_x.copy()
       step = min(max_step, new_step)
```

Let's design `optimize_T` carefully:
```
def optimize_T(a_init, max_time, p=4, rng=None):
  n=len; fsize; a=normalize; y=a; step=0.05/sqrt(n)
  best_T=inf; best_a; best_M=inf; best_Ma

def _next_fsize(n): # full conv size 2n-1

`project_simplex_fast(v,S=1.0)`:
```
v=np.asarray(v,float)
if v.size==0: return v
if v.min() >= -1e-12:
   v=np.maximum(v,0); s=v.sum(); if s>0: return v*(S/s)
# exact
w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
css=np.cumsum(w)
idx=np.arange(1,v.size+1,dtype=float)
cond = w > (css - S)/idx
if not np.any(cond):
   # If S very small? theta = (v.sum()-S)/n?
   theta=(np.sum(v)-S)/v.size
else:
   rho=int(np.max(np.where(cond)[0]) + 1)
   theta=(css[rho-1]-S)/rho
x=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
# adjust tiny negatives and sum
s=x.sum()
if s > 0 and abs(s-S) > 1e-12*max(1,S): x*=S/s
return x
```
Need standard algorithm condition `w > (css - S)/rho`? Actually valid if theta < w_k. The formula for rho: largest k such that w_k > (cumsum[w[:k]] - S)/k. Floating; using >= maybe. Our code okay. If no cond, theta=(sum(v)-S)/n? The standard if no valid (θ ≥ all w), then rho=n? Actually if S < sum(v) and large positive? For nonnegative projection, there is always a solution. If all v negative and S=1, no cond? Example v=-1, -1, S=1: w=-1; (css-S)/idx =(-1-1)/1=-2, w>-2 true; cond. Fine.

if len(w) < fsize:

return 2.0*g
```

Let's write `optimize_T` in more detail:
```
def optimize_T(a_init, max_time, p=4.0, use_momentum=True, rng=None):
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(987)
    n=len(a_init)
    if n<2: return a_init,0,None,0
    a0=normalize_sum(a_init,1.0)
    if max_time <= 0:
       M=conv_nonneg(a0).max(); return a0,M,a0,0
    fsize=_next_fsize(n)
    start=time.monotonic()
    y=a0.copy(); prev=a0.copy(); t=1.0
    step=0.05/np.sqrt(n)
    min_step=1e-14/np.sqrt(n)
    max_step=0.25/np.sqrt(n)
    best_T=np.inf; best_M=np.inf; best_Ta=a0.copy(); best_Ma=a0.copy()
    it=0; last_improve=0; no_accept=0
    stuck_limit=max(100, int(3000*?))? Use 200.
    while time.monotonic()-start < max_time:
       it+=1
       b=conv_nonneg(y)
       T=np.sum(b**p)
       M=b.max()
       if T < best_T*(1-1e-12): best_T=T; best_Ta=y.copy(); last_improve=it
       if M < best_M*(1-1e-12): best_M=M; best_Ma=y.copy(); last_improve=it
       if T <= 0: break
       w = b**(p-1) if p!=1 else np.ones_like(b)
       ws=w.sum()
       if ws <=0: w=np.zeros_like(b); w[np.argmax(b)]=1
       else: w=w/ws
       g=grad_from_weights(y,w,fsize)
       g=g-g.mean()
       gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gn < 1e-15: break
       d=-g/gn
       accepted=False
       # choose radii
       mults=(1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125)
       for mult in mults:
          r=step*mult
          if r>max_step: r=max_step
          if r<=0: break
          x=project_simplex_fast(y+r*d,1.0)
          bx=conv_nonneg(x)
          Tx=np.sum(bx**p)
          MX=bx.max()
          # accept if T decreases significantly and M not explosion?
          if Tx < T*(1-1e-9):
             accepted=True; new_x=x; new_step=min(max_step,max(min_step,r))
             break
       if not accepted:
          no_accept+=1
          step *=0.5
          if step < min_step or no_accept>50:
             # escape
             frac=min(1.0,(time.monotonic()-start)/max_time)
             noise=0.02*(1-0.5*frac)/np.sqrt(n)
             y=project_simplex_fast(best_Ta + rng.normal(0, noise, n),1.0)
             prev=y.copy(); t=1.0; step=0.05/np.sqrt(n); no_accept=0; last_improve=it
             continue
          continue
       no_accept=0
       if use_momentum:
          t_new=0.5*(1.0+np.sqrt(1.0+4.0*t*t))
          y= new_x + ((t-1.0)/t_new)*(new_x - prev)
          y=np.maximum(y,0.0)
          s=y.sum()
          if s>0: y=y*(1.0/s)
          else: y=project_simplex_fast(new_x,1.0)
          # This scaling not exact projection but fine; for exact use project if any? Use project_simplex_fast to satisfy nonneg/sum.
          y=project_simplex_fast(y,1.0)
          prev=new_x.copy(); t=t_new
       else:
          y=new_x.copy(); t=1.0
       step = new_step
       if it % 10 == 0 and verbose? no
    # Final check best_Ma
    return best_Ma, best_M, best_Ta, best_T
```

def make_inits(n, rng, prev=None):
    inits=[]
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    if prev is not None: inits.append(resample_sequence(prev,n))
    inits.append(np.ones(n))
    # two-step
    a=np.ones(n); a[t<0.5]+=0.333; inits.append(a); a2... ascending
    # linear descend
    inits.append(1.0 - 0.7*t + 0.1) # ensure pos
    inits.append(0.5 + 0.5*np.sin(np.pi*t)) etc
    # random smooth
    r=1.0 + 0.4*np.sin(2*np.pi*rng.uniform(1,5)*t + rng.uniform(0,2)) + 0.2*np.cos(...)
    inits.append(np.maximum(r,0.05))
    # exponential
    inits.append(np.exp(-2*t)+0.2)
    # block: 3 blocks [1.6,1.0,0.4]
    for vals in ...:
    return [np.maximum(x,0) for x]
```

Resample/dowsample:
```
def resample_sequence(a, L):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if len(a)==L: return a
    if len(a)==0: return np.ones(L)
    old_t=np.arange(len(a))/len(a)
    new_t=np.arange(L)/L
    b=np.interp(new_t, old_t, a, left=a[0], right=a[-1])
    return np.maximum(b,0)
```
For downsample from larger, interpolation may not be L1 average but okay.

Could use periodization? No.

Now, `full_optimize` details with T ratio and candidates:
```
def full_optimize(a_init, max_time, rng, T_ratio=0.4, max_p_start=8, max_p_end=128):
  start=time.monotonic(); end=start+max_time
  a0=normalize_sum(a_init,1.0)
  best_a, best_M = min objective from a0
  cands=[a0]
  t_T = max_time*T_ratio
  if t_T > 5:
      a_tm, M_tm, a_tt, Tn_tt = optimize_T(a0, t_T, p=4.0, use_momentum=True, rng=rng)
      cands.append(a_tm); cands.append(a_tt)
      # update best with M_tm
  # dedup cands
  kept=[]
  for c in cands:
     if not any(np.linalg.norm(c-k)<1e-5 for k in kept if same len): kept.append
  # remaining
  rem=(end - time.monotonic()) - 10
  if rem <=0: return best
  # split: weights 0.7,0.2,0.1
  for i,a in enumerate(kept):
      if rem<=0 break
      wgt=[0.6,0.3,0.1][i] if i<3 else 0
      seg=wgt*rem
      if seg<2: continue
      a_m, M_m = optimize_max(a, seg, p_start=max_p_start, p_end=max_p_end, use_momentum=False, rng=rng)
      if M_m<best_M: best...
      rem -= seg
  # if still rem>10, final max on best
  if rem>10:
      a_m,M_m=optimize_max(best_a, rem-5,...)
  return best_a,best_M
```

Let's detail `make_weights_from_b(b,p)`:
```
def make_weights_from_b(b,p):
    M=b.max()
    L=b.size
    if M <= 0:
       return np.ones(L)/L
    bn=b/M
    if p <= 1.0:
       w=np.ones(L)
    else:
       w=bn**(p-1.0)
    s=w.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
       w=np.zeros(L); idx=int(np.argmax(b)); w[idx]=1.0
    else: w = w/s
    return w
```
For p large, bn** maybe underflow; okay. If all zeros, use top.

`tn_value(b,p)`:
```
M=b.max(); if M<=0 return 0; bn=b/M; return float(np.sum(bn**p))
```

In `optimize_T`, p can be fixed; maybe if p=4, b**p for Tn? We use tn_value. For weights use make_weights.

`optimize_T` code final:
```
def optimize_T(a_init, max_time, p=4.0, use_momentum=True, rng=None):
  if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(1)
  n=a_init.size
  if n==0: return ...
  a0=normalize_sum(a_init)

  fsize=...

  best_Tn=np.inf; best_M=np.inf; best_Ta=a0.copy(); best_Ma=a0.copy()

       continue

    if gn<1e-15: break
    d=-g/gn

    radii = (step, step*0.5, step*0.25, step*0.125, step*0.0625)
    for r in radii:

Dedup helper:
```
def candidates_unique(cands):
  out=[]
  for c in cands:
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    if c.size==0: continue
    dup=False
    for k in out:
       if k.size==c.size and float(np.linalg.norm(c-k)) < 1e-5:
          dup=True; break
    if not dup: out.append(c)
  return out
```

def generate_inits(n, rng, prev=None):
   out=[]
   t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
   if prev is not None:
      out.append(resample_sequence(prev,n))
   out.append(np.ones(n))
   a=np.ones(n); a[t<0.5]+=0.4; out.append(a)
   a=np.ones(n); a[t>=0.5]+=0.4; out.append(a)
   a=1.5 - 1.0*t; out.append(a)  # 1.5 to0.5
   a=0.5 + 1.0*t; out.append(a)
   a=1.0 + 0.35*np.sin(2*np.pi*rng.uniform(0.2,1.0)*t + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)) + 0.2*np.cos(4*np.pi*t)
   out.append(np.maximum(a,0.05))
   out.append(np.exp(-2.0*t)+0.1)
   # three blocks
   b=np.empty(n)
   b[t<1/3]=1.7; b[(t>=1/3)&(t<2/3)]=1.0; b[t>=2/3]=0.4
   out.append(b)
   # random after FFT smoothing? simple
   r=1.0 + 0.5*rng.normal(0,0.4,n)
   # smooth by convolution with box
   kernel=np.ones(5)/5
   r=np.convolve(r,kernel,mode='same')
   out.append(np.maximum(r,0.05))
   # normalize and clip
   res=[]
   for x in out:
      x=np.asarray(x,float); x=np.maximum(x,0.0); s=x.sum()
      if s>0: x=x/ s else uniform
      res.append(x)
   return res[:8]
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2

Approach:
- Normalize sum(a)=1.
- Use low-resolution fluctuation + peak-polishing to find a good shape.
- Upsample to multiple candidate lengths.
- Refine with two complementary descent loops:
  1) T-phase: reduce normalized sum of b_k^p (p=4) to flatten the convolution.
  2) M-phase: reduce max(b) using high-power active-peak subgradients.
- Keep the best sequence found and return it as a clipped non-negative list.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import rfft, irfft


def next_fsize(n):
    """Next power of two at least 2*n-1."""
    size = 2 * int(n) - 1
    if size <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()


def conv_nonneg(a, fsize=None):
    """Full self-convolution, clipped to remove tiny negative FFT noise."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    if fsize is None:
        fsize = next_fsize(n)
    A = rfft(a, fsize)
    c = irfft(A * A, fsize)
    c = c[:size]
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def objective_J(a):
    """Objective for a raw sequence, using the evaluator's clamping rules."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    b = conv_nonneg(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * b.max())


def normalize_sum(a, S=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(a.size, S / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (S / s)


def project_simplex_fast(v, S=1.0):
    """Project onto {x >= 0, sum(x) = S}. Fast path when already non-negative."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size == 0:
        return v.copy()

    if v.min() >= -1e-12:
        v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
        s = float(v.sum())
        if s > 1e-300:
            return v * (S / s)
        return np.full(v.size, S / max(1, v.size), dtype=np.float64)

    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, v.size + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta_arr = (css - S) / idx
    valid = w > theta_arr
    if np.any(valid):
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) + 1)
        theta = theta_arr[rho - 1]
    else:
        rho = v.size
        theta = (float(v.sum()) - S) / v.size

    x = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s > 1e-300 and abs(s - S) > 1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(S)):
        x = x * (S / s)
    return x


def grad_from_weights(a, w, fsize):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w_k * b_k with b = a*a.
    w has length about 2*len(a)-1.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    wp = w
    if wp.size < fsize:
        tmp = np.zeros(fsize, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(wp.size, fsize)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = wp[:m]
        wp = tmp
    else:
        wp = wp[:fsize]

    A = rfft(a, fsize)
    W = rfft(wp, fsize)
    g = irfft(W * np.conj(A), fsize)
    return 2.0 * g[:n]


def make_weights_from_b(b, p):
    """Normalized weights proportional to b_k^(p-1), safe for large p."""
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    L = b.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L

    bn = b / M
    if p <= 1.0:
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = np.power(bn, p - 1.0)

    s = float(w.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = int(np.argmax(b))
        w[idx] = 1.0
    else:
        w = w / s
    return w


def tn_value(b, p):
    """Normalized peakiness sum((b/max(b))^p)."""
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if b.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    M = float(b.max())
    if M <= 0.0:
        return 0.0
    bn = b / M
    if p <= 1.0:
        return float(bn.size)
    return float(np.sum(np.power(bn, p)))


def optimize_T(a_init, max_time, p=4.0, use_momentum=True, rng=None):
    """
    Flatten convolution by reducing T = sum((b/M)^p).
    Also tracks best max(b) as a safety net.
    """
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)

    a0 = normalize_sum(a_init, 1.0)
    n = a0.size
    if n <= 1:
        M = float(conv_nonneg(a0).max()) if n > 0 else 0.0
        return a0, M, a0, 0.0

    if max_time <= 1e-6:
        b = conv_nonneg(a0)
        return a0, float(b.max()), a0, tn_value(b, p)

    fsize = next_fsize(n)
    start = time.monotonic()

    y = a0.copy()
    prev = a0.copy()
    tcur = 1.0
    step = 0.05 / np.sqrt(float(n))
    min_step = 1e-12 / np.sqrt(float(n))
    max_step = 0.25 / np.sqrt(float(n))

    best_Tn = float("inf")
    best_M = float("inf")
    best_Ta = a0.copy()
    best_Ma = a0.copy()

    it = 0
    last_improve = 0
    no_accept = 0

    while time.monotonic() - start < max_time:
        it += 1
        b = conv_nonneg(y)
        M = float(b.max())
        if M <= 0.0:
            break
        Tn = tn_value(b, p)

        if Tn < best_Tn * (1.0 - 1e-9):
            best_Tn = Tn
            best_Ta = y.copy()
            last_improve = it
        if M < best_M * (1.0 - 1e-9):
            best_M = M
            best_Ma = y.copy()
            last_improve = it

        if it - last_improve > 160:
            frac = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / max_time)
            noise = 0.025 * (1.0 - 0.6 * frac) / np.sqrt(float(n))
            y = project_simplex_fast(best_Ta + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n), 1.0)
            prev = y.copy()
            tcur = 1.0
            step = 0.05 / np.sqrt(float(n))
            no_accept = 0
            last_improve = it
            continue

        w = make_weights_from_b(b, p)
        g = grad_from_weights(y, w, fsize)
        g = g - g.mean()
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if not np.isfinite(gn) or gn < 1e-15:
            break

        d = -g / gn
        accepted = False
        new_x = None
        step_acc = step

        step_candidates = (
            step,
            step * 0.5,
            step * 0.25,
            step * 0.125,
            step * 0.0625,
        )

        for r in step_candidates:
            r = min(max(r, 0.0), max_step)
            if r <= 0.0:
                continue
            x = project_simplex_fast(y + r * d, 1.0)
            bx = conv_nonneg(x)
            Mx = float(bx.max())
            if Mx <= 0.0:
                continue

            # Track any improvement even if not used as next y.
            if Mx < best_M * (1.0 - 1e-9):
                best_M = Mx
                best_Ma = x.copy()
                last_improve = it

            Txn = tn_value(bx, p) if Mx <= M * 1.05 else float("inf")
            if Txn < best_Tn * (1.0 - 1e-8):
                best_Tn = Txn
                best_Ta = x.copy()
                last_improve = it

            accept = False
            if Mx < M * (1.0 - 1e-8):
                accept = True
            elif Mx <= M * 1.02 and Txn < Tn * (1.0 - 1e-6):
                accept = True

            if accept:
                accepted = True
                new_x = x
                step_acc = r
                break

        if not accepted:
            no_accept += 1
            step *= 0.5
            if step < min_step or no_accept > 60:
                frac = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / max_time)
                noise = 0.02 * (1.0 - 0.5 * frac) / np.sqrt(float(n))
                y = project_simplex_fast(best_Ta + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n), 1.0)
                prev = y.copy()
                tcur = 1.0
                step = 0.05 / np.sqrt(float(n))
                no_accept = 0
                last_improve = it
                continue
            continue

        no_accept = 0
        step = min(max(step_acc, min_step), max_step)

        if use_momentum:
            tnew = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * tcur * tcur))
            y = new_x + ((tcur - 1.0) / tnew) * (new_x - prev)
            y = project_simplex_fast(y, 1.0)
            prev = new_x.copy()
            tcur = tnew
        else:
            y = new_x.copy()
            tcur = 1.0

    return best_Ma, best_M, best_Ta, best_Tn


def optimize_max(a_init, max_time, p_start=8.0, p_end=128.0, use_momentum=False, rng=None):
    """Reduce max(convolution) directly using active high-power subgradients."""
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(54321)

    a0 = normalize_sum(a_init, 1.0)
    n = a0.size
    if n <= 1:
        b = conv_nonneg(a0)
        return a0, float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0

    if max_time <= 1e-6:
        b = conv_nonneg(a0)
        return a0, float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0

    fsize = next_fsize(n)
    start = time.monotonic()

    y = a0.copy()
    prev = a0.copy()
    tcur = 1.0
    step = 0.04 / np.sqrt(float(n))
    min_step = 1e-12 / np.sqrt(float(n))
    max_step = 0.20 / np.sqrt(float(n))

    best_M = float("inf")
    best_a = a0.copy()

    it = 0
    last_improve = 0
    no_accept = 0

    while time.monotonic() - start < max_time:
        it += 1
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        b = conv_nonneg(y)
        M = float(b.max())
        if M <= 0.0:
            break

        if M < best_M * (1.0 - 1e-10):
            best_M = M
            best_a = y.copy()
            last_improve = it

        if it - last_improve > 220:
            frac = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / max_time)
            noise = 0.02 * (1.0 - 0.7 * frac) / np.sqrt(float(n))
            y = project_simplex_fast(best_a + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n), 1.0)
            prev = y.copy()
            tcur = 1.0
            step = 0.04 / np.sqrt(float(n))
            no_accept = 0
            last_improve = it
            continue

        progress = min(1.0, elapsed / max_time)
        p = p_start * (max(p_end, p_start) / p_start) ** progress
        w = make_weights_from_b(b, p)
        g = grad_from_weights(y, w, fsize)
        g = g - g.mean()
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if not np.isfinite(gn) or gn < 1e-15:
            break

        d = -g / gn
        accepted = False
        new_x = None
        step_acc = step

        step_candidates = (
            step,
            step * 0.5,
            step * 0.25,
            step * 0.125,
            step * 0.0625,
        )

        for r in step_candidates:
            r = min(max(r, 0.0), max_step)
            if r <= 0.0:
                continue
            x = project_simplex_fast(y + r * d, 1.0)
            bx = conv_nonneg(x)
            Mx = float(bx.max())
            if Mx < best_M * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                best_M = Mx
                best_a = x.copy()
                last_improve = it
            if Mx < M * (1.0 - 1e-9):
                accepted = True
                new_x = x
                step_acc = r
                break

        if not accepted:
            no_accept += 1
            step *= 0.5
            if step < min_step or no_accept > 60:
                frac = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / max_time)
                noise = 0.015 * (1.0 - 0.7 * frac) / np.sqrt(float(n))
                y = project_simplex_fast(best_a + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n), 1.0)
                prev = y.copy()
                tcur = 1.0
                step = 0.04 / np.sqrt(float(n))
                no_accept = 0
                last_improve = it
                continue
            continue

        no_accept = 0
        step = min(max(step_acc, min_step), max_step)

        if use_momentum:
            tnew = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * tcur * tcur))
            y = new_x + ((tcur - 1.0) / tnew) * (new_x - prev)
            y = project_simplex_fast(y, 1.0)
            prev = new_x.copy()
            tcur = tnew
        else:
            y = new_x.copy()
            tcur = 1.0

    return best_a, best_M


def resample_sequence(a, L):
    """Resample a shape to length L using linear interpolation on [0, 1)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    L = int(L)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == L:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(L, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)

    old_t = np.arange(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    new_t = np.arange(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    b = np.interp(new_t, old_t, a, left=a[0], right=a[-1])
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        b = b / s
    else:
        b = np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    return b


def generate_inits(n, rng, prev=None):
    """Several nonnegative shape initializations."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return []

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    raw = []

    if prev is not None and prev.size > 0:
        raw.append(resample_sequence(prev, n))

    raw.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[t < 0.5] += 0.35
    raw.append(a)

    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[t >= 0.5] += 0.35
    raw.append(a)

    a = 1.4 - 0.8 * t
    raw.append(a)

    a = 0.6 + 0.8 * t
    raw.append(a)

    freq1 = rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0)
    freq2 = rng.uniform(1.5, 3.0)
    ph1 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
    ph2 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
    a = 1.0 + 0.35 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq1 * t + ph1) + 0.2 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq2 * t + ph2)
    raw.append(a)

    raw.append(np.exp(-2.0 * t) + 0.1)
    raw.append(0.5 + np.sin(np.pi * t) + 0.3 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t))

    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[t < 1.0 / 3.0] = 1.6
    a[(t >= 1.0 / 3.0) & (t < 2.0 / 3.0)] = 1.0
    a[t >= 2.0 / 3.0] = 0.45
    raw.append(a)

    a = 1.0 + 0.45 * rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, n)
    if n > 8:
        kernel = np.ones(5, dtype=np.float64) / 5.0
        a = np.convolve(a, kernel, mode="same")
    raw.append(a)

    out = []
    for x in raw:
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if x.size != n:
            x = resample_sequence(x, n)
        x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
        s = float(x.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            x = x / s
        else:
            x = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        out.append(x)
        if len(out) >= 9:
            break
    return out


def candidates_unique(cands):
    """Remove almost duplicate candidate sequences."""
    out = []
    for c in cands:
        c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if c.size == 0:
            continue
        dup = False
        for o in out:
            if o.size == c.size:
                diff = float(np.linalg.norm(c - o))
                if diff < 1e-5:
                    dup = True
                    break
        if not dup:
            out.append(c)
    return out


def full_optimize(a_init, max_time, rng, T_ratio=0.35, max_p_start=8.0, max_p_end=128.0):
    """
    Run T-phase and max-phase on one length, returning best-by-max(b).
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    a0 = normalize_sum(a_init, 1.0)
    n = a0.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), float("inf")

    b0 = conv_nonneg(a0)
    best_M = float(b0.max())
    best_a = a0.copy()

    if max_time <= 5.0:
        if max_time > 1.0:
            a_m, M_m = optimize_max(
                a0, max_time,
                p_start=max_p_start, p_end=max_p_end,
                use_momentum=False, rng=rng
            )
            if M_m < best_M:
                best_M = M_m
                best_a = a_m.copy()
        return best_a, best_M

    J0 = 2.0 * n * best_M
    if J0 > 1.60:
        T_ratio = max(0.40, T_ratio)
    elif J0 > 1.55:
        T_ratio = max(0.30, T_ratio)
    else:
        T_ratio = min(0.25, max(0.15, T_ratio))

    t_T = float(max_time) * float(T_ratio)
    cands = [a0]

    if t_T > 8.0:
        a_tm, M_tm, a_tt, _ = optimize_T(
            a0, t_T,
            p=4.0,
            use_momentum=True,
            rng=rng
        )
        if np.isfinite(M_tm) and M_tm < best_M:
            best_M = M_tm
            best_a = a_tm.copy()
        cands.append(a_tm)
        if a_tt is not None and a_tt.size == n:
            cands.append(a_tt)

    cands = candidates_unique(cands)

    rem = (start + float(max_time)) - time.monotonic() - 8.0
    if rem <= 5.0:
        return best_a, best_M

    scored = []
    for c in cands:
        cc = normalize_sum(c, 1.0)
        Mc = float(conv_nonneg(cc).max())
        scored.append([Mc, cc])

    # Small manual sort by M; avoid lambda.
    for i in range(len(scored)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(scored)):
            if scored[j][0] < scored[i][0]:
                tmp = scored[i]
                scored[i] = scored[j]
                scored[j] = tmp

    if len(scored) == 1:
        segs = [rem]
    elif len(scored) == 2:
        segs = [0.68 * rem, 0.32 * rem]
    else:
        segs = [0.58 * rem, 0.27 * rem, 0.15 * rem]

    for i in range(len(scored)):
        seg = float(segs[i]) if i < len(segs) else 0.0
        if seg < 5.0:
            continue
        a_m, M_m = optimize_max(
            scored[i][1], seg,
            p_start=max_p_start, p_end=max_p_end,
            use_momentum=False, rng=rng
        )
        if np.isfinite(M_m) and M_m < best_M:
            best_M = M_m
            best_a = a_m.copy()

    tail = (start + float(max_time)) - time.monotonic() - 5.0
    if tail > 10.0:
        a_m, M_m = optimize_max(
            best_a, tail,
            p_start=max(max_p_start, 16.0), p_end=max(max_p_end, 192.0),
            use_momentum=False, rng=rng
        )
        if np.isfinite(M_m) and M_m < best_M:
            best_M = M_m
            best_a = a_m.copy()

    return best_a, best_M


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = t0 + float(max(60, budget_s) - 70)

    print("Start. budget=%.1fs" % float(budget_s))

    prev = None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if arr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(arr.sum())
            if s > 0.0:
                prev = arr / s
    except Exception:
        prev = None

    if prev is not None:
        best_a = prev.copy()
        M = float(conv_nonneg(best_a).max())
        best_J = float(2.0 * best_a.size * M)
        print("Given sequence length=%d J=%.6f" % (best_a.size, best_J))
    else:
        best_a = np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64)
        best_a /= best_a.sum()
        M = float(conv_nonneg(best_a).max())
        best_J = float(2.0 * best_a.size * M)
        print("No given sequence; starting uniform length=8192 J=%.6f" % best_J)

    total_work = max(30.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic())

    # Cheap low-resolution shape search.
    low_budget = min(160.0, 0.065 * total_work)
    if total_work > 300.0 and low_budget > 40.0:
        Nlow = 512
        inits = generate_inits(Nlow, rng, prev)
        inits = inits[:6]
        if len(inits) > 0:
            per = low_budget / float(len(inits))
            for inx in inits:
                if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 120.0:
                    break
                if per < 8.0:
                    per = min(per, max(0.0, (hard_deadline - 120.0) - time.monotonic()))
                if per <= 3.0:
                    break
                alo, M_lo = full_optimize(
                    inx, per, rng,
                    T_ratio=0.42,
                    max_p_start=6.0,
                    max_p_end=96.0
                )
                if alo.size > 0:
                    J_lo = float(2.0 * alo.size * M_lo)
                    if np.isfinite(J_lo) and J_lo < best_J:
                        best_J = J_lo
                        best_a = alo.copy()
                        print("  low-n %d improved J=%.6f" % (alo.size, best_J))
                    per = low_budget / float(max(1, len(inits) - inits.index(inx) - 1)) if inits.index(inx) < len(inits) - 1 else 0.0

    # Candidate lengths.
    lengths = []
    if prev is not None and 1024 <= prev.size <= 32768:
        lengths.append(int(prev.size))

    for L in [32768, 16384, 8192, 4096]:
        if L not in lengths:
            lengths.append(L)

    # Keep at most three; prioritize first (prev) and larger lengths.
    if len(lengths) > 3:
        keep = [lengths[0]]
        for L in lengths[1:]:
            if L not in keep:
                keep.append(L)
        keep.sort(key=None)  # no-op placeholder; sort manually below
        # Manual stable-ish sort by descending length for the tail.
        tail = keep[1:]
        for i in range(len(tail)):
            for j in range(i + 1, len(tail)):
                if tail[j] > tail[i]:
                    tail[i], tail[j] = tail[j], tail[i]
        lengths = [keep[0]] + tail[:2]
    else:
        lengths = lengths[:3]

    total_remaining = max(30.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic())
    weights = []
    for L in lengths:
        weights.append(0.55 + 0.50 * (np.log2(max(2.0, float(L))) / np.log2(32768.0)))
    wsum = float(sum(weights))
    if wsum <= 0.0:
        wsum = 1.0

    for idx, L in enumerate(lengths):
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 80.0:
            break

        wgt = weights[idx] / wsum
        t_alloc = 0.97 * total_remaining * wgt
        if t_alloc < 35.0:
            continue

        if best_a.size == L:
            start_seq = best_a.copy()
        elif prev is not None and prev.size == L:
            start_seq = prev.copy()
        elif best_a.size > 0:
            start_seq = resample_sequence(best_a, L)
        elif prev is not None:
            start_seq = resample_sequence(prev, L)
        else:
            start_seq = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
            start_seq /= start_seq.sum()

        print("Length %d: start J=%.6f, allocate %.1fs" % (
            L, objective_J(start_seq), t_alloc
        ))

        a_opt, M_opt = full_optimize(
            start_seq, t_alloc, rng,
            T_ratio=0.32,
            max_p_start=8.0,
            max_p_end=160.0
        )

        if a_opt.size > 0 and np.isfinite(M_opt):
            J_opt = float(2.0 * a_opt.size * M_opt)
            print("Length %d: best J=%.6f" % (L, J_opt))
            if J_opt < best_J:
                best_J = J_opt
                best_a = a_opt.copy()

    # Final polish with safe remaining time.
    tail_budget = hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 25.0
    if tail_budget > 20.0 and best_a.size > 0:
        a_fin, M_fin = optimize_max(
            best_a, tail_budget,
            p_start=16.0, p_end=256.0,
            use_momentum=False, rng=rng
        )
        if a_fin.size > 0 and np.isfinite(M_fin):
            J_fin = float(2.0 * a_fin.size * M_fin)
            if J_fin < best_J:
                best_J = J_fin
                best_a = a_fin.copy()
        print("Final polish J=%.6f" % best_J)

    # Safety fallback.
    if best_a is None or best_a.size == 0 or best_J >= 1000.0 or not np.isfinite(best_J):
        best_a = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64)
        best_a /= best_a.sum()
        best_J = objective_J(best_a)

    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(best_a)):
        best_a = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64)
        best_a /= best_a.sum()

    # Normalize to sum 1 for return; objective is scale invariant.
    s = float(best_a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        best_a = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(best_a.sum())
    best_a = best_a / s
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    # Avoid pathologically tiny max if something went wrong.
    if best_a.size > 0:
        bcheck = conv_nonneg(best_a)
        Mcheck = float(bcheck.max())
        Jcheck = float(2.0 * best_a.size * Mcheck)
        if not np.isfinite(Jcheck) or Jcheck > 1.55:
            # Keep, but if evaluator-oriented objective is bad, use uniform fallback.
            if Jcheck > 2.5:
                best_a = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64)
                best_a /= best_a.sum()
                Jcheck = objective_J(best_a)

    ans = [float(x) for x in best_a]
    # Final defensive check for Python typing.
    for i in range(len(ans)):
        if not np.isfinite(ans[i]) or ans[i] < 0.0 or ans[i] > 1000.0:
            ans[i] = 1.0 / max(1, len(ans))
    sfinal = float(sum(ans))
    if sfinal < 0.01:
        ans = [1.0 / max(1, len(ans))] * max(1, len(ans))
    else:
        inv = 1.0 / sfinal
        for i in range(len(ans)):
            ans[i] *= inv

    print("Returning length=%d internal J=%.6f" % (len(ans), objective_J(ans)))
    return ans
```
